# TLC results

Generated by `tools/tlc_report.py`. Errors are the true lock error (detuning minus mrm's lock reference), world 0, scored from `score_from_s`. The p99.9 / peak / rms columns come from the recorded 10 us-mean detuning trace; `track_*` is the same error with the TLC's dither tones removed (least squares per 4 ms block; TLC runs only). The mrm columns (`max_abs_lock_error_pm` onwards) are mrm's own per-tick metrics. Thresholds: mrm 5 pm budget, ECE496 2 GHz. Corner runs with corner priors (`experiment@corner`): the plant and every controller's nominal knowledge follow the corner, as mrm treats every model-based controller, so a corner tests the TLC on a different plant it knows, not a model mismatch. Corner runs with nominal priors on the corner plant (`experiment@corner (nominal priors)`): the plant follows the corner and every controller keeps the g26 design (the TLC its G26 priors), a model mismatch.
